VALIDATION / CAUTIONS

Complete original question-set coverage: 678 unique decision IDs exactly matching 678 unique manifest IDs. Distinct underlying text locations: 582.
New decisions since prior GitHub backup: 156. Changed earlier decisions: 0. Missing earlier decisions: 0.
All 678 records contain id, choice, detail, note, corrected_text and updated_at as strings, with valid Unicode/UTF-8. All full-word Kit/Sefaria choices have saved corrected_text. The optional new_edition_count_wrong field is preserved.
No original scan crops are provided in this decisions export. Recognition/print-chipping hypotheses are derived from the user's saved decisions and handwritten notes; they cannot prove the physical cause of a mark without scanning the original.
Publishing a backup on GitHub does not itself synchronize the website's device-local IndexedDB answer database; no reviewer application code was changed in this update.

SUMMARY COUNTS

Decisions 678 / 678 (100% question coverage). Distinct text sites: 582. New since previous: 156.
Choices: use_kit 96, use_sefaria 22, other 28, other_sign 4, unclear 4, count:0 206.
Records with notes: 96. Whole-word selections with corrected_text: 118. Flagged newer-edition count errors: 43.
Zero mark counts: meteg 90, dot_above 32, dot_inside 21, patah 28, dot_below 6, revia 6, munah 8.
Identified pattern categories: 27. Categories overlap and pattern example totals must not be summed.

MAIN NEW FINDINGS

• Completed all 678 review questions; all previous 522 choices preserved.
• False/absent meteg remains largest count:0 category, with 90 records; the source of all 90 misdetections is not proven.
• Mark grouping matters: two points can constitute one sheva, nine points three segols (s17205, s31634).
• Revia can be absent or share a location with a real cholam; preserve ambiguity as shown by the new notes.
• Newly reported reconstruction display issues affect letter placement, colon/punctuation and niqqud positioning.
• New-edition count disagreement flags rise to 43, and should not be mistaken for proven historical edition differences.

PATTERN 01 — False metegs / over-detection
Interpretation: The completed 678-question export has 90 meteg records answered count:0 out of 137 meteg questions (48 zero counts in the prior 522-answer snapshot). This is the largest zero-sign cluster. These answers mean that the reviewer chose zero at those specific locations; they do NOT prove all 90 arose from one printing/chipping mechanism.
Suggested review / OCR rule: Investigate whether suggested meteg marks actually appear in the scan, separating missing marks, nearby munach, letter strokes, and source-transfer issues. Avoid claiming a common cause without original crops.
